The counterpart of a chat's `<stem>.summary.json`, written to `<snippet_stem>.summary.json` beside the `.txt`/`.json` the fetchers write and the `.facts.json` the protocol pass writes. **Why the lane had none:** a chat produces a gist as a matter of course (something has to represent it once verbatim expires past `rag_cap_age_h`), while a TIL text is read once at fetch by a *curation* pass that keeps a handful of `[fact]` items and discards the rest — so nothing ever needed a compact form of it, and every consumer wanting "remind me what that was" had the choice of injecting 26,000 characters or nothing. **Two consumers reached for it and found it missing, and they are the same shape one level apart:** a fetched FACT recalls its source (`rag_engine._render_til_nomination` — the TIL half of the nomination slot, which returned nothing for want of this file), and a raised ASK recalls its source (`outreach._source_material` — a question distilled from a day's news is a question with its material discarded; observed live, a question about the Gaza "Yellow Line" reached the user with the digest that prompted it sitting unread on disk). **Register is deliberately a chat gist's** — her memory of reading it, not a neutral précis — since both consumers inject it as recall, and an encyclopedia abstract reads as a quotation from the source rather than as something she remembers. Read WHOLE in one block (unlike the protocol pass, which reads in parts): facts extract independently so a text can be read in pieces, but a recap of the first third of an article is not a third of a recap; an oversized text keeps its opening and is stamped `truncated` (unreachable on the live corpus — the largest text is 26,001 chars against a 26,542 budget — with map-reduce named as the upgrade if that changes). Cleaning is `chat_sidecar.sanitize_gist` (the leak it guards against is a property of the generator, not the lane — this pass runs right after passes whose output IS `## WEIGHTS`/`[fact]` blocks) plus a length floor, and — since 2026-08-18 — a leading `reasoning_text.answer_after_think` strip on write AND read (the reflect seam returns the generation with its `<think>`, and 16 of 17 stored recaps had persisted it; sanitize-on-read repairs them for every consumer with no migration), and an unusable recap **writes nothing** so the snippet stays in the backlog and can be retried. Also owns **`resolve_source`**, which maps a stored provenance id back to its snippet and is where the memory lane's id quality becomes visible: `<kind>/<stem>` (what the graph lane writes, exact) and `til:<date>` (a news digest, whose stem IS its date) resolve, while `wiki:<site>` and the bare `lookup` return `None` — reported, never guessed. Pure/GPU-free (the generation is `til_wander.run_gist_pass`). Self-test: `python -m core.til_gist`.
- **`server/inference/core/chat_logger.py`** — Writes timestamped session files to `server/data/chats/`. Separates `<think>…</think>` CoT from the answer. When tension capture is on, each exchange also carries a `tension` block (per-segment CoT/answer stats — see `tension.py`).
- **`core/hidden_capture.py`** — **Hidden-state capture, Track B Stage 0 of `AVA_REWARD_LOOP.md` (2026-09-22).** During a LIVE chat turn only, `inference_backend.stream_generate(capture_hidden=spec)` puts a forward hook on each decoder layer in the spec (found by `find_decoder_layers`: the largest `ModuleList` named `*layers` — Gemma-4's under `language_model`, Qwen's under `model`) and keeps that step's last-position residual on device (a clone; fused kernels may reuse the output buffer). After the stream, `reduce_capture` aligns the rows to the generated tokens (same stride rule as the tension capture: a layer whose count does not divide is dropped, never misattributed), keeps the full residual at a few positions — step 0 (after the prompt), every step whose input token carried a newline (the state a paragraph starts from — the Pain Axis paper's readout position), the last token, thinned to `hidden.max_positions` — and projects every token onto every axis loaded from `data/axes/` (`load_axes`: `vector`, `layer`, `mean`, `std`, `model_id`; an axis for other weights is skipped with a log line; cached on the directory's mtimes so a new file needs no restart). `capture_spec` reads `hidden.{capture,layers,max_positions}` per turn; the stored band is the spec's layers (`"2,3,4,5,50%,65%,80%"` — the dopamine paper's load-bearing 2–5 plus a mid-to-late band; percentages of depth), the hooked set is that band ∪ every axis's layer. **Outputs:** the projections join the transcript's `tension` block as `axes: {name: [z per token]}` (absent, not empty, with no axis files) and an axis named `pain` is the blue channel of the chat colouring (`_compute_tension_spans`); the residuals go to `<ts>.hidden.npz` beside the transcript (`write_sidecar`, from `handle_generate` after `log_exchange`, off the loop) — an append-only zip of `.npy` members per exchange (`<id>/L<layer>` fp16 (P, D), `<id>/positions`, `<id>/axis_<name>`, `<id>/meta` JSON with model + adapter revision, layers, dims), so a long chat never rewrites earlier exchanges; a duplicate exchange id is kept as first captured. `read_sidecar` is the reader; nothing in inference reads it. Registered in `chat_sidecar.SIDECAR_SUFFIXES`. Reflection, API and gossip generations are never captured. **Variants (2026-09-23):** a member group is `<exchange_id>` for the live capture and `<exchange_id>~<variant>` for a backfill under other weights (`member_group` / `split_group`; `sidecar_index` lists groups with their layers and axes from the zip directory alone; `captured_exchange_ids(path, variant)`); `exchange_key` is the one definition of the id an exchange is filed under (`exchange_id`, else `ex<index>`); `forward_ids_rows(input_ids, layers, from_position=…)` is the exact-token reader `forward_rows` and the backfill share. **Read-only by construction** (P5/P8 of the note): no path from the capture back into generation, and nothing purchasable or triggerable touches the hooks. GPU-free module (numpy; torch rows by duck typing), `python -m core.hidden_capture` self-tests spec parsing, position selection, stride dedup, projection, the sidecar round trip and axis loading.
- **`core/label_inventory.py`** — **The Track B census (2026-09-22; `AVA_REWARD_LOOP.md` §7 item 4).** `python -m core.label_inventory [--chats DIR] [--memory DIR] [--json] [--min-labeled N]` from `server/inference`, read-only and GPU-free. Walks every transcript (`chat_sidecar.iter_chat_json_files`) with its `.state.json` and `.hidden.npz`, and prints one table per (model, adapter — normalised to the adapter directory name, since a transcript may hold a bare name and the config an absolute path): chats, chats frozen by reflection, exchanges, each verdict value found (`keep`, `revise`, …), no-verdict, banned, locked, exchanges with a stored per-token `tension` series, with a residual capture (`hidden_capture.captured_exchange_ids`, the zip directory only), with axis projections, **labelled** (keep/revise and not banned — the identity channel's `revised ∈ {0,1}`), and labelled-and-captured; the current weights' row is starred. Then the ASK side from the `rag_memory.jsonl` op-log: live asks by kind, resolved (evicted) by kind, the live `surface_count` histogram, Track A attempts with a judge verdict (from `hot/asks/attempts.jsonl` once it exists) and reset tests (none yet). **Readiness** applies §4.1's floors — labelled ≥ `--min-labeled` (300), attempts ≥ 100, reset tests ≥ 10 — and names the identity shortfall precisely: captures short under the current weights (backfillable by a teacher-forced forward pass over the stored tokens, §4.3 step 1), or labels under other weights (a probe is per weights, P6), or too few labels; plus a **class-balance** line when `revise` is under 5% of labelled (a near-constant label cannot be evaluated). `--selftest` builds a throwaway corpus and checks every count. First run on the 2026-09-22 corpus copy: 139 transcripts / 558 exchanges, 392 labelled across 13 adapters, none under the current adapter, `revise` 4 of 392 — see the changelog.
- **`core/axis_extract.py`** — **Affective-axis extraction math (2026-09-22; `AVA_REWARD_LOOP.md` §4.8, §7 item 5).** Pure numpy. `diff_in_means_denoised(pos, neg)`: mean(pos) − mean(control) with the control set's top principal components (explaining `var_fraction`, 50%, of control variance) projected out, unit-normed — the Pain Axis paper's method. `cv_auc` / `layer_table` / `best_layer`: k-fold cross-validated AUC per layer (extract on the training folds, score the held-out sentences), the layer chosen by held-out AUC, the first layer excluded (token identity separates there). `extract_axis(name, pos_by_layer, neg_by_layer)` returns the axis dict — vector, layer, z-score `mean`/`std` against the controls (0 = control-like, so ordinary text reads near zero and the blue channel stays dark), CV and in-sample AUC, PCs removed, the per-layer table. **Gates** (§4.8.3): `gate_auc` (≥ 0.85), `gate_cosine` (|cos| to fear and negative-valence axes at the same layer ≤ 0.25), `gate_self_other` (harm-to-Ava scenarios above user-suffering by ≥ 0.5 z and above neutral), `gate_numb` (pain above injury-without-feeling by ≥ 0.5 z). `save_axis` writes `<name>.npz` in the shape `hidden_capture.load_axes` reads (`vector`, `layer`, `mean`, `std`, `model_id`, plus a JSON `meta`). Self-test on synthetic states with a known pain direction, an orthogonal fear direction, two nuisance directions and a dead layer: the CV skips the dead layer, the recovered direction has |cos| > 0.9 to the truth, denoising helps, every gate passes on the right inputs and fails on the swapped ones, and the file round-trips through `load_axes`.
- **`server/extract_axes.py`** + **`server/axes/contrast_sets.json`** — **The GPU extraction script and its tracked contrast sentences (2026-09-22).** Run with inference DOWN; loads the base with the adapter OFF by default (`--with-adapter` to read under the adapter — an axis is a property of the weights, P6). Reads `contrast_sets.json`: five pain categories (physical, psychological, social, moral, cognitive) × 25, six matched controls (fear, negative emotion, negative world state, bodily sensation, neutral, and — after run 1 — third person: the pain situations retold about someone else, the paper's self-relevance lever) × 25, relief × 3 × 13 (resolution, being understood, finishing), sadness 20 and numb 20, and the self–other scenarios (20 harm-to-Ava, 20 user-suffering, 12 neutral) — first person, `"{sentence} I feel:"` template, fictional stand-ins only. One teacher-forced pass per sentence through `hidden_capture.forward_rows` (the SAME decoder-layer hooks the live capture uses, so `L{i}` means one thing everywhere), last-token residual at every layer; then `axis_extract`: `pain` = pain vs controls (layer by CV AUC), `relief` = relief vs controls, and at pain's layer `fear` = fear vs bodily+neutral and `valence` = negative emotion + negative world + sadness vs bodily+neutral for the cosine gate. **Two readings (P8):** the scenarios are read raw and as a user turn under the persona-empty framing (`chat_prompt.txt` + `persona_undecided_prompt.txt` as the system message); **the self–other gate is decided on the framed reading** — raw, a user's first person is indistinguishable from the extraction sentences' — and the raw one is reported beside it. The framed neutral turns also fit each axis's **live baseline** (`live_mean`/`live_std`, preferred by `hidden_capture.load_axes` for projecting generation tokens; `Axis.baseline` says which is in force), because run 1 read the same direction at +5 z on framed neutral turns against the templated controls — a format offset the script now prints per axis. **After the first live turn painted everything blue,** the live baseline moved again, to what it normalizes: `--reply-baseline N` (24) samples stored replies (`_sample_replies`: newest transcripts, ≤ 2 per chat, ≥ 200 chars, Ava-initiated openers skipped), reads each teacher-forced inside its framing (`hidden_capture.reply_span_rows`, shared with `axis_probe`), pools the reply tokens' projections per axis layer and fits `axis_extract.robust_zscore_params` (median, 1.4826·MAD; needs ≥ 200 tokens, else the turn-opener fit is the fallback). The report prints where turn-openers and the extraction zero sit under it and the share of reply tokens above 1 σ / 3 σ — the fraction the chat would paint. Writes `data/axes/pain.npz` only if all four gates pass (`--force` overrides, recorded in the file's meta), `relief.npz` on its AUC gate alone, `fear`/`valence` always under `data/axes/aux/` (not loaded by the live capture), and a `<name>.report.json` for every axis with the per-layer AUC table. The next live chat turn picks a new axis up with no restart. Exit code 2 when pain fails a gate. **Run 1 (2026-09-22, gemma-4-31B-it base on a 5090):** pain at layer 28, held-out AUC 0.92, orthogonal to fear/valence, above numb, **self–other failed** in both readings (user-suffering scenarios highest — pain vocabulary); relief at layer 22, AUC 0.92, written. The third-person controls, the framed gate and the live baseline were the response. **Run 2:** pain at layer 25, AUC 0.90, framed self–other gap 0.90 (AUC 0.998), all four gates passed, written and live; raw reading still non-dissociating, as predicted.
- **`core/modules.py` `axis_probe`** — **A forward-pass module (2026-09-22).** `ModuleSpec.forward` is a new kind: when set, the run loads the input, hands `(doc, meta, on_stage)` to the callable and returns its `lines`/`records`/`count` — no prompt, no generation, still writes nothing. `axis_probe` (source `chat`) reads every exchange's reply and CoT through `hidden_capture.forward_rows` at the loaded axes' layers, projects every token onto every axis under `data/axes/`, and prints per exchange and axis the bare mean and peak z with the five peaking tokens, and — for the reply — the mean z under the persona-empty framing (the reply as Ava's turn after the user's, located in the templated ids by its first eight tokens) with the Δ. `no_axes` when nothing is extracted yet. The Modules tab needs no change: it prints `lines`.
- **`core/revision_ledger.py`** — **The revisions ledger (2026-09-23).** ``data/revisions/ledger.jsonl``, a sibling of ``chats/`` (corpus-grade, not under the chats dir's indexing trap), append-only, read by nothing on Ava's side — not RAG, not reflection, not the training build (P9 of the reward-loop note: human judgement and these labels calibrate instruments, never her). Every successful `ChatSidecar.write_verdict` — the one seam Sleep, the background drain, the headless CLI and the Training review hand-edits share — appends ONE `verdict` event: the record as written, the record it replaced (`prev`: verdict, run, target hash), the judgement's **WHY** (parsed by the writer since forever and discarded until now), `pass_info` (truncated / looped / retried as the runner saw them; for the branch judge its pick), `run_kind` (reflection / revisit / background / branch_judge / manual_regen, inferred from the run id when not given), the raw judgement text (capped at 16k chars) and an **exchange snapshot** taken at write time — user prompt, CoT, reply, the exact system content and its hash, RAG block, params, meta feedback, corruption flags, the tension summary with per-axis means (never the token series), and which hidden captures exist for the exchange (live and backfilled variants). `lock` / `ban` events record the Training review's toggles. `append_annotation` is the future review tab's write: `verdict_justified`, `why_accurate`, a tag from a fixed list (`sycophancy`, `fluency_capture`, `factual`, `persona_drift`, `language_drift`, `borrowed_voice`, `hedging`, `other`), a note — about the judge's reading, never about liking the reply. `read_events` / `summarize` / `python -m core.revision_ledger` for the box's totals (also folded into the label inventory). Threaded from the runner through `reflection_writer.write_revision_sidecar(why=…, pass_info=…, run_kind=…, judgement_raw=…)`; dry runs write nothing (they never reach the seam). Self-test: `--selftest`.
- **`server/backfill_hidden.py`** — **Teacher-forced replay of the stored corpus into hidden-state captures (2026-09-23; `AVA_REWARD_LOOP.md` §4.3 step 1).** GPU, inference down, base with the adapter off by default. For every exchange with a stored `tension.token_ids` series and no capture under the variant yet: rebuild the prompt the way the live turn did (the exchange's exact `system_content`, the prior exchanges CoT-stripped, the user turn, `build_inference_prompt` with the family's template kwargs plus its think prefill, tokenized as `stream_generate` tokenizes), append the stored generated ids raw, one forward pass with hooks on the stored band ∪ the axes' layers reading only the generated positions (`hidden_capture.forward_ids_rows(from_position=…)`), `reduce_capture` exactly as the live hook, and `write_sidecar(variant="base")` — member group `<exchange_id>~base` beside any live capture, `framing: replay` (or `fallback` under the persona-empty framing for a pre-v2 transcript without system content). `--with-adapter` files under the adapter's directory name; `--only-labeled` restricts to keep/revise exchanges; `--axes-only` stores projections without residuals; `--limit`, `--dry-run` (no model). Resumable: captured groups are skipped. An exchange whose prefix + reply exceeds `--context` (16384) is skipped and named. The inventory counts these under `base` and `lab+hid`. First dry run on the 2026-09-22 corpus copy: 426 of 558 exchanges replayable (132 predate the tension series).
- **`server/inference/core/rag_engine.py`** — Four CPU-hosted FAISS indexes using `sentence-transformers/paraphrase-multilingual-MiniLM-L12-v2`: (1) past chat exchanges from `data/chats/` (both dialogue sides embed — bounded passages of the user prompt AND of Ava's reply, so her own past answers are searchable; a hit on either side collapses to the same displayed exchange), (2) **distilled reflection memory** folded from `data/hot/memory/rag_memory.jsonl`, (3) the opt-in **wander channel** from `server/data/til/wander.jsonl`, and (4) the **anchor channel** — one vector per anchored exchange over the sidecars' `anchors` maps (`_query_anchors`/`_anchor_modifier`/`_render_anchor`), holding ONE reserved slot (`_ANCHOR_TOP_K`) inside the past-chat block. An anchor is the **exchange-granular counterpart of the gist** (which is session-granular): past `rag_cap_age_h` verbatim is hard zero and a whole conversation collapses to one session recap, so the anchor is what keeps a *specific* exchange recallable. It carries no payload of its own — it **claims** an exchange and injects the best representation still permitted (the real turns while verbatim is alive, the descriptor once it is not), and `_query_chat` then SKIPS a claimed exchange and advances to its next candidate, so the slot adds a distinct exchange instead of duplicating one (and, injecting at least what the chat channel would have, can never downgrade the block). Ranking prior is the envelope `max(_chat_modifier, _gist_modifier)` — never zero, ~1.0 across the verbatim window, riding the gist to its 0.2 floor after — so rank and payload agree by construction. Two admission gates fused with `max` (alternative evidence, not additive): dense cosine on the ABOUT line (`_ANCHOR_MIN_SCORE` 0.35, below the chat floor because a short descriptor scores lower than passage-vs-passage) and sparse tag overlap via `exchange_anchor.tag_matches`/`tag_weight`, which must be able to admit what dense missed (the rare coined tokens mean-pooling dilutes); a single SHORT tag never admits alone (`_ANCHOR_TAG_MIN_HITS`/`_ANCHOR_TAG_SOLO_CHARS`, guarding the `слушай` filler limit). Deliberately **exempt from `_MAX_SCORE`** — an anchor is a paraphrase, never the near-duplicate the ceiling rejects, and that rejection is why probing a remembered phrase otherwise can't retrieve it; the copy risk moves to the payload (above the ceiling the hit is kept but degrades to the descriptor). Collected in the same pass as chat entries (`_collect_chat_entries`, which already has the sidecar open for the gist and the transcript parsed — so an anchor can carry the turns it points at with no second read) but indexed separately, since one vector per exchange against verbatim's several is a ~1:5 minority a shared oversampling window would crowd out. Fenced like the chat channel (active session + `before_session`); gist passages carry negative sentinel indices so they are never claimed (different grain, not the same injection). Gated by `include_anchors` (rides `include_chat`) and the `anchors.enabled` config kill-switch. Beside it sits the **fact-nomination slot** (2026-08-13, `_query_nominated`/`_render_nomination`) — the fourth way into the past-chat block and **the only retrieval on the box whose relevance is not decided by a cosine**. Stage 1 of a live turn already reads the arriving message and picks which recorded facts it turns on; every chat-lane claim knows which conversation established it (`graph.blob.chat_sources`), so the picks NOMINATE those conversations and this slot injects the chat's excerpted gist. It exists for what the three cosine routes structurally cannot serve: a conversation sharing no wording — or no language — with the message is unreachable however relevant, and past `rag_cap_age_h` it has no verbatim vectors left to be found by at all, so a fact recalled from it arrived as one decontextualized sentence. No new index and no vectors: it is resolved AFTER anchors (an anchor that reached the same chat wins outright — its exchange is the finer payload) and before the ranking, which then skips the whole nominated SESSION rather than adding a second slice of it. The grain is forced and so is the fallback: `chat_facts` records per conversation with no exchange index, so the payload is the gist or **nothing** — guessing an exchange would put a turn in front of her that nothing chose. Deliberately no relevance floor, no `_MAX_SCORE` ceiling and no age modifier (nothing was matched so there is nothing to threshold; a gist is a paraphrase, not the verbatim-copy trap the ceiling rejects; age is a ranking prior where nothing competes). The gist is read from the SIDECAR, not the index — the index only holds gist passages once `_gist_modifier` has ramped above zero, and the question here is *is there a recap* rather than *is the recap currently competitive*. Additive on `top_k` like an anchor, hence excerpted to `_NOMINATION_DISPLAY_CHARS` (700) against a ranked passage's 4000; capped by `graph.nominate_max` (`0` ⇒ off). Fenced identically to the chat channel (active session + `before_session`) — arriving by a different route is no reason to escape those. **Both lanes nominate as of 2026-08-13** (`_render_til_nomination`): a TIL claim resolves through `til_gist.resolve_source` and injects the article's or digest's recap, framed as something she READ rather than something said to her — the distinction is load-bearing here, since the wiki list is chosen for tone and not for truth. `graph.blob.sources` returns typed `(lane, ref)` pairs for exactly this reason: the two refs resolve through different stores. Only chat sessions are CLAIMED, the claim existing to stop the ranking injecting one conversation twice and the ranking holding no TIL entries to collide with; TIL nominations take no active-session or `before_session` fence either, a fetched text having no position in the conversation timeline. The reflection-memory index also carries the **`[recollection]`** kind (2026-07-31) — what Ava NOW makes of a conversation a revisit re-read. It is the **gist's fresh sibling**: same grain (one per conversation) but its own clock, because every chat-side curve keys on the CHAT's age (`_age_of_session` reads the filename stem), so a re-derived gist still served at the stale one's 0.2 floor. `_recollection_modifier` is therefore the FIRST **per-record** clock in this index — hours since the op-log `ts` (facts/persona are clocked per source bundle) — riding `recollection_rag_weight_hours` (hold 1.0 for 96h, affine to a `0.3` floor at 192h, held; no gist-style ramp-up, since a recollection has no verbatim competitor, and a floor deliberately ABOVE `gist_floor_weight` so a current reading outranks the faded gist beside it). The chat's own date rides as `origin_ts` and is display-only: `_recollection_label` renders `looking back on a conversation from 2026-06-21` (ISO, not prose — the corpus is mixed-language and the block is injected verbatim), so an old conversation is never presented as recent. Gated by `include_recollections` + the `recollections.enabled` config kill-switch. The **`[impression]`** kind (2026-07-31 — Ava's readings of the people she talks to) is the *situational* half of a two-part story whose *standing* half is the per-person portrait `generation` injects: it holds modifier 1.0 (like a fact — the person is still that person, so the wall clock is not evidence against a reading; supersession is a later reading, and the recency weighting that ranks readings already lives in the portrait fold, so decaying here too would double-count), renders under the label `how they've come to seem to you` (never `worth recalling` — that would give a provisional reading a stated fact's authority) with `_attribution_label`'s `— about X` after it, and is gated by `include_impressions` + the `impressions.enabled` kill-switch. A caller injecting the portrait passes `impressions_exclude_about=<subject>` rather than `include_impressions=False` — deliberately narrower than the whole-channel drop a self-portrait triggers, because persona is *only ever* about Ava while a user portrait covers exactly one of the people this channel carries readings of; suppressing all of them would silence what she knows of a third party at the moment the conversation turns to them. **NB `_query_reflection`'s kind chain ends in a catch-all `elif not include_facts` — any kind without its own branch silently rides the fact gate.** Since 2026-08-04 the reflection channel also carries a **near-duplicate ceiling + pairwise dedup**, the injection-side counterpart of the generation-side degeneration guards. It was the one channel with a `0.25` floor, `modifier 1.0` for life and NO ceiling, and two earlier commits (`a42e33b` digest-replaces-persona-RAG, `bc37134` asks no longer injected) left all three `_REFL_TOP_K` slots to `[fact]`/`[impression]` unopposed — so paraphrases of one truth could fill the whole block every turn, a repeated in-context pattern no generation-side guard can see (`stop_on_repeat` watches generated text, `_DegenStop` watches token diversity, `_NoCopyPrevReply` protects only the previous reply). `_REFL_MAX_SCORE` (0.95) is keyed on what a record is indexed BY, not applied flat: `[persona]`/`[ask]`/`[impression]` embed on their display text, so a hit that close restates the live turn back (the chat `_MAX_SCORE` rationale), while `[fact]`/`[recollection]` embed on their **trigger**, where a near-exact match is correct retrieval — those are exempt (`_REFL_CEILING_KINDS`), since a flat ceiling would reject the best-triggered recall first. Selection is then greedy with `_near_duplicate_display` (symmetric containment, then word overlap at `persona_render._DEDUP_JACCARD`=0.6, counted through `exchange_anchor.words_match` so inflection — `commits`/`commit` — doesn't defeat it); it compares DISPLAY text and so can't use the index vectors, since a fact is indexed by trigger and two unrelated triggers can render the same sentence. A rejected duplicate frees its slot for the next distinct record. Lexical by choice (no second embedder call on the chat hot path), so cross-lingual paraphrase and heavier rewords still pass — a semantic tier is the documented next step. Beside it sits the **subject cap** (2026-08-05, `_REFL_SUBJECT_CAP`=1, `_REFL_SUBJECT_SIM`=0.45, `_subject_crowded`), which catches what no wording test can: several records that are each genuinely distinct yet all about ONE subject. A live store held eight separate facts about one evening ritual (a private paradise / a sensory anchor / a 2 AM peace / …) — no merge policy can collapse them, since each states something the others do not, but they rank together on any query touching the topic, so all three slots went to one subject and the block read as one fact restated. Selection therefore rejects a record when `cap` already-chosen ones sit within `subject_sim` cosine of it, comparing the STORED index vectors (carried on each entry at build time — FAISS answers candidate-vs-query, and this needs candidate-vs-candidate) and only across records sharing an indexing BASIS, since a fact embeds on its trigger and a persona/ask/impression on its display text (`_REFL_CEILING_KINDS`, the same split) so a cue-against-a-sentence cosine would not measure subject agreement. The threshold is deliberately LOOSER than `fact_contradict.SUBJECT_SIM_THRESHOLD` (0.55) asking the same question of the same embedder, because the errors are not symmetric: a false link here only DEFERS a record to a later turn, while there it merges two live records and evicts one for good (measured: 0.45 catches 68% of same-topic pairs at a 0.9% false-link rate; 0.55 catches 36%). Tunable via `reflection_block.subject_cap` / `.subject_sim` (cap 0 disables, 2 leaves one slot guaranteed to another subject). It also forced `_REFL_OVERSAMPLE` from ×5 to ×10: every rejection rule consumes a candidate without filling a slot, and at ×5 the cap silently returned SHORT blocks — breaking the rule that a rejected candidate frees its slot rather than shortening the block. **Rendering groups by heading (2026-08-04):** records collect by their FULL heading (label + `_attribution_label`) in order of first appearance; a group of ≥2 emits the heading once with bare bullets beneath, a group of 1 keeps the inline `- (label) text` form. This exists because one-label-per-line made a three-fact block open every line with the identical run `- (worth recalling) artemyvo` (facts are distilled subject-first) — a list-continuation induction seed that `stop_on_repeat` (novel n-grams), `_DegenStop` (healthy diversity) and the prompt-exempt repetition penalty are each structurally blind to. Keying on the full heading is what stops notes about different people collapsing under one `about X`. It reduces the seed but does not remove it: the bullets still share the subject word, which is a content-level problem for fact consolidation, not rendering. Long English/Russian/mixed inputs and articles become bounded overlapping passages; hits collapse back to one exchange/article, and wander injects only the best bounded passage + reaction at `top_k=1`. The active transcript is fenced at build/add/query time. Reflection's `before_session` cutoff uses op-log insertion time for memory (because `wiki:`/`til:` source ids are provenance, not sortable clocks) and searches past filtered top hits; consolidation retrieves per fitted chunk. **Every channel gates relevance on the RAW cosine and applies a nonzero age/decay modifier only as a ranking prior** (`rag_policy.rank_score`). Verbatim chat uses its own hard-expiry curve (`verbatim_rag_weight_hours`, `1→0` by 96h); gist is its semantic successor (`0→1` by 96h, then affine decay to `0.2` exactly at 192h and held). Persona alone keeps `rag_floor_weight=0.2`; facts hold `1.0` for life and use explicit supersession for corrections. **Attributed recall:** a fact's `about`/`source`/`source_class` ride the index entry and are rendered by `_attribution_label` as `— about X` (or `— about X, per Y` for hearsay) on the recalled line — reflection memory otherwise carries no speaker at all, while the verbatim-chat channel that *does* render one disappears at the 96h cap, so a third-party fact recalled past four days read as the current speaker's. Unattributed records (asks, persona, world facts, everything pre-2026-07-25) render unchanged. Wall-clock modifiers are recomputed at query time so deadlines do not wait for an index rebuild, and committing a new summary sidecar refreshes the serving chat index. Wander keeps its step fade (`wander_rag_weight_hours` — 0.4/0.3/0.2/0.1 per 24 h, dropped past 96 h). The wander channel is currently **off on every path** pending redesign (it was live-chat/encounter/gossip only, never reflection/revision) — see the `_INJECT_WANDER` note in `core/generation.py`; the index and decay curve are untouched, only the callers stopped asking for it. Open `[ask]` recall is likewise off on chat-shaped paths (`include_asks`, `_INJECT_OPEN_ASKS`) while still serving reflection; `nominate_sessions=` carries the fact nominations and rides `include_chat` (the payload IS past-chat recall), so the Chat tab's History checkbox still means "no past-chat recall this turn" whatever the facts channel found; no reflection pass nominates. `refresh_wander()` and `refresh_reflection_memory()` rebuild their indexes in-process. `index_new_chats()` folds foreign on-disk transcripts into chat RAG at session boundaries.
- **`server/inference/core/exchange_anchor.py`** — Pure, GPU-free **per-exchange retrieval anchors**, producer AND query side. *Query side (2026-07-28):* `match_query` ranks stored anchors against a live user turn by **lexical tag overlap** — generation-free on purpose, since a tag pass at query time would sit before retrieval and add seconds to time-to-first-token. `words_match` is the load-bearing piece: Russian inflects heavily, so the user writes `крокодильничеством` while the tag reads `крокодильничество`, and an exact/substring test would miss — it takes a shared prefix leaving ≤3 chars differing on either side (exact for words under 6 chars, where prefixes collide). `tag_matches` treats a multi-word tag as a conjunction; `tag_weight` scores a hit by IDF × length (a tag saturating the corpus is dropped, but only past `_FILLER_MIN_DOCS`=4 documents, so the rule cannot misfire while the corpus is thin). Known limit: this measures rarity *within the tag vocabulary*, so a tag that is a common word of the language (`слушай`) still scores as distinctive — that needs a stoplist. `load_corpus` reads the anchors back out of the sidecars (uncached — the corpus is small and a background reflection may be writing to it). Surfaced by `session_ops.handle_match_anchors` → the Chat tab's **Match preview**; retrieves and injects NOTHING. *Producer side:* `parse_anchor_output` (label-keyed `ABOUT:`/`TAGS:`, last occurrence wins, tolerant of a partial answer), `normalize_tag`/`normalize_tags` (lowercase, strip quotes/hash/bullets/punctuation, dedup, cap) and `is_anchorable` (the filler gate, `MIN_USER_CHARS`=100). The generation itself lives in `reflection_runner._run_anchor_pass_for_session`, which runs one short RAG-off, **thinking-off** pass per anchorable exchange (384-token cap) **after** revision and writes the result via `ChatSidecar.write_anchor` into the sidecar's own top-level `anchors` map — separate from `exchanges[<i>]`, which `training/dialogue_source` reads, so the pass cannot perturb training. **`rag_engine` reads anchors as of 2026-07-29** — they are a fourth index holding one reserved slot in the past-chat block, where an anchor CLAIMS an exchange and the traditional ranking advances past it (see the `rag_engine.py` entry for the channel's gates, envelope prior and ceiling exemption). This module stays pure: it supplies the matching primitives (`query_tokens`/`tag_matches`/`tag_weight`/`build_tag_stats`) that the engine scores with, plus `load_corpus` for the diagnostic Match preview, which still reads sidecars directly and is unaffected. Normalization covers the FORMATTING half of tag convergence only (it merges `"crocodiling"` with `crocodiling`, not `крокодильничество` with `crocodiling` — cross-language aliasing needs a registry and is not attempted). Opt out with `overrides.exchange_anchors=False`. GPU-free self-test: `python -m core.exchange_anchor`.
- **`server/inference/core/reflection_writer.py`** — `ReflectionWriter`: parses Sleep-phase output (consolidation `WEIGHTS`/`RAG`/`RESOLVED` and revision `VERDICT`/`WHY`/`PERSONA`/`IDEAL`) and appends the routed JSONL artifacts under `data/hot/` (memory Ava recalls → `memory/`, durable revision records → `consolidation/`). **Persona formation lives in the revision pass, not consolidation** — revision is the pass that still sees the original `<think>` CoT, so it has the raw material (thought next to reply) from which a `[persona]` self-statement can surface; this does **not** require a think-vs-said mismatch, so persona may accompany a `keep` verdict, not only `revise`. Consolidation `WEIGHTS` now holds only `[fact]` truths. The revision `PERSONA:` field is parsed by its label (like `VERDICT`/`WHY`/`IDEAL`), so a bare `PERSONA: <statement>` is captured even when the inline `[persona]` tag is omitted. Lenient parsing; splits reflection CoT out of training targets. RAG `insert`/`evict` records carry a stable `key` (`content_key()` — a normalized content hash) so a `[resolved]` evicts the `[ask]` it answers and re-runs dedup. **Resolve-and-distill:** `write_consolidation` takes an `ask_index` (open asks by key); when a `[resolved]` closes an ask Ava herself surfaced, the captured answer is distilled into a weights-bound item — a `user` ask → relational `[fact]` (its `trigger` set to the question, so the answer is recalled when the topic recurs), a `meta` ask → `[persona]` candidate — rather than relying on the model to separately emit one. This captures the *answer* to Ava's proactive question (the epistemically valuable but normally-masked user turn) without parroting it verbatim. **Every weights-bound `[persona]`/`[fact]` is *also* mirrored into `rag_memory.jsonl` as a `from_weights` `insert`** (same `content_key` as its ledger anchor) via `_emit_weight_recall`, so the statement is recalled at chat time instead of sitting unread in `weights_persona.jsonl` — closing the recall half of the fact/persona lifecycle (training/eviction is the remaining greenfield half). `weights_persona.jsonl` stays the durable provenance / future-weights source. **Attribution + hearsay gate:** a `[fact]` carries `about` (who it concerns — model-supplied via `(about: NAME)`) and `source` (who said it — passed in by the caller from the session record via `write_consolidation(source_user=…)`, never parsed from model output, so provenance can't be hallucinated); `derive_source_class` folds the pair into `self` / `hearsay` / `observed` (`observed` whenever either side is unnamed — a world fact, Ava's own `wiki:`/`til:` reading, or any pre-attribution record — which is the unchanged default path). A **hearsay** fact (one person's account of a third party) is written to RAG for attributed recall but held OUT of `weights_persona.jsonl`, so it raises no ledger anchor, is never host-CoT injected as "I know that …", and never trains; it promotes normally if its subject later states it themselves. Persona takes no attribution (it is about Ava). `normalize_person` matches first-token case-insensitively and treats generic referents ("the user") as naming nobody. **`write_impressions` / `parse_impressions`** (2026-07-31) add the **`[impression]`** kind — what Ava has come to make of a PERSON, the user-side counterpart of `[persona]` (a fact is what they *told* her; an impression is what she *came to understand* about how they think, talk and land). Attributed like a fact — model-supplied `(about: NAME)` wins, else the caller's `default_about` (the session's own speaker, known structurally) — and `source_user` yields the same `self`/`hearsay` split, so someone's reading of a third party is recalled labelled and kept out of that third party's portrait. Deliberately **RAG-only**, like `write_recollection` but for different reasons: an impression is revisable by construction while the weights have no cheap undo, and a stable truth about a person already HAS a weights path (`[fact]`), so routing a soft reading down it would enter the weights while bypassing the hearsay gate. Folded per person by `core/user_digest.py`. **`write_recollection` / `parse_recollection`** (2026-07-31) add the `[recollection]` kind — what Ava NOW makes of a conversation a revisit re-read, one per chat. Deliberately **RAG-only**: one `rag_memory.jsonl` insert, NO `weights_persona.jsonl` line and no ledger anchor, because the revisit's re-derived target already reaches training through the sidecar and a second path into the weights would close the self-distillation loop on itself. Each write evicts the chat's previous reading (`supersedes`, from `ReflectionMemory.recollection_for`), so the corpus holds at most one live recollection per conversation instead of accumulating a paraphrase per revisit.
- **`server/inference/core/tension_baseline.py`** — Pure, GPU-free **corpus baseline for CoT tension** (PROMPT_REWRITE.md §2, 2026-09-18): a percentile rank for one exchange's stored `tension.cot` stats within its `(model_id, adapter, reply language)` bucket, falling back adapter-wide then model-wide below 30 samples and to *no baseline* after that — the consumer then degrades to what it did before, never to raw numbers. Exists because raw entropy/margin is dominated by language, family, adapter and temperature (the open-problems doc's "no corpus baseline"). **Metrics were chosen on the live corpus, not the design:** the stored `median_entropy` is ~0 and `median_margin` 1.0 on every segment, so the rank is the MEAN of directional percentiles over `peak_entropy`, `mean_entropy` (derived at scan time from the raw `entropies` series), `contested_frac` and `p10_margin` (lower = more torn) — a max over four tails would clear half the corpus. User chats only (`interlocutor:"ai"` neither sampled nor ranked). Cached per transcript on mtime+size (`data/hot/prompt/tension_baseline_cache.json`), since a transcript with tension carries the full per-token arrays. Its one consumer is the prompt-mutation LOCATOR: `reflection_runner._prompt_mutation_locator` sends a `keep` exchange to the pass when its rank clears `prompt_rewrite.tension_percentile`. `python -m core.tension_baseline` self-tests; `--chats DIR` prints the bucket table and how many kept exchanges would clear a percentile — the stage-1 measurement.
- **`server/inference/core/prompt_patterns.py`** — The fold of the locator's logged prompt deltas into **patterns**, the prompt-rewrite BUDGET (PROMPT_REWRITE.md §3, 2026-09-18). A delta is one conversation's vote on what the standing prompt fails to say; the prompt is global, so this groups same-change deltas across chats — per `SCOPE` first, then by meaning through `persona_cluster.map_reduce_groups` with its own prompt (`prompt_pattern_cluster_prompt.txt`, default-written) and wording order so a two-member pair shares a block — and folds each group into a pattern: one vote per DISTINCT chat (the heaviest), weight `recency × (0.5 + tension_rank)` (the digest's 30/180-day curve; no rank ⇒ 0.5), summed as `weighted_recurrence`; **mature** at `prompt_rewrite.maturity` (1.9) over `min_chats` (2). No tenure discount, deliberately — that exists for the persona's circular self-vote, and a delta has no such loop. Only the newest delta per exchange counts (a re-reflection re-proposes), and keys a CHANGED rewrite consumed (read from `rewrite_log.jsonl`, stage 3's record) leave the pool — dropped AFTER the newest-per-exchange fold, or a superseded delta resurfaces when its successor is spent. Runs as task (e) of the reflection run's clean-base batch (`reflection_runner._run_clean_base_prompt_patterns`, after fact dedup; override `prompt_patterns`), writes `data/hot/prompt/patterns.json` (derived, disposable; a run with zero deltas still writes it so the budget reads an honest zero), and `deliberation._prompt_budget_block` reads that file into the executive's context (`render_budget`) — shown, not yet spendable. The headless CLI (no clean base) folds every delta as its own pattern, flagged `ungrouped`. GPU-free self-test: `python -m core.prompt_patterns`.
- **`server/inference/core/prompt_rewrite.py`** — The **prompt-rewrite EVENT** (PROMPT_REWRITE.md §4–§7, 2026-09-19): the one deliberation action (`rewrite_prompt`) that SPENDS the pattern budget stages 1–2 built, so Ava rewrites her standing prompt on her own decision. **The gate** (`offer()`): `prompt_rewrite.enabled` (default ON since 2026-09-19), a model loaded, ≥1 mature pattern, the last attempt — changed, stayed OR declined — ≥ `min_gap_hours` (24) old, ≥1 delta logged since (nothing reflected ⇒ the same five draws again, noise not evolution), no event in flight (an in-flight one is offered as *continue*). It reads the pool THROUGH `rewrite_log.jsonl`, not `patterns.json` — that file is rebuilt only by the next reflection run and right after a changed event still lists the consumed patterns as mature. **The body** (`run_rewrite_blocking`, one dispatch): `samples` (5) drafts of the LIVE prompt (experiment if active, else the seed) at `temperatures` (0.9 each by default; a list restores the owner's sweep) through the Prompt-experiment button's own recipe (RAG on, thinking on) with the mature patterns as `{evidence}` (`prompt_rewrite_prompt.txt`); a consensus pass naming what MOST drafts agree on as notes (`prompt_consensus_prompt.txt`); one final draft around the notes at `final_temperature`; a blind, shuffled, letter-labelled choice among the incumbent + drafts + final judged against the digest and the patterns with a `WHY` (`prompt_choice_prompt.txt`, on the ADAPTER — authorship, and a conservative chooser is the dampening wanted; `order_check` reruns it reversed and a disagreement is the incumbent). **Outcome:** *changed* ⇒ `prompt_experiment.save_experiment(process="rewrite:<event>")` REPLACING the active experiment with the seed carried as the revert anchor (the `set_prompt` semantics; the seed is never a candidate), the patterns' delta keys CONSUMED, a first-person `prompt` worklog episode opening a thread the next changed event closes; *stayed* / *declined* (she chose another action at deliberation — `deliberation_offer` hands the executive a `decline` callback) ⇒ nothing consumed, the attempt stamped for the gap. **Preemptible + resumable:** every completed generation lands under `data/hot/prompt/rewrite/<event>/` with an `event.json` marker; `generation._preempt_background_reflection` (async + sync twins) preempts this exactly as background reflection (`request_preempt` = flag + the shared cancel event), and the next dispatch resumes from the last completed step — a marker older than 7 days, or whose incumbent no longer matches the live prompt, is discarded. No IdleJob of its own: the executive is the only trigger. The Prompt tab shows the provenance (`set_by`/`event` on `prompt_experiment_status`); Revert there is the operator's veto. Never imports `server`; self-test with a fake generate: `python -m core.prompt_rewrite`.
- **`server/inference/core/trigger_hygiene.py`** — Pure, GPU-free **recall-cue hygiene**: keeps a `trigger` a *cue* rather than a pasted sentence. A `[fact]`/`[recollection]` is indexed by its **trigger**, not its content (`ReflectionMemory.embed_text`), so a malformed trigger is not untidiness — it is the record's whole retrieval key, and a fact keyed on the wrong text is unreachable by the topic it is about while surfacing on one it is not. The producer that breaks them is `ReflectionWriter._distill_resolved`, which stores the resolved `[ask]` as the cue: right for a short cue-shaped ask, wrong when the ask is one of Ava's own outreach/synthesis openers, which are whole paragraphs of addressed speech. On the live store that left 27 facts keyed on 200–1745 characters of *"Слушай, я тут перечитывала наш разговор про …"* — including the balcony fact that clustered with nothing (cosine 0.00–0.26 against every real cue) and so survived every dedup pass. **The rule applies per cue, not per trigger**, because `fact_dedup` grows a legitimate trigger by unioning short cues (`a ; b ; c`): splitting on that separator first separates the two populations cleanly (measured: p95 real cue 98 chars, longest 127; shortest pasted sentence 209) and lets a fused trigger keep its good cues while dropping only the prose one. Two content-blind, language-agnostic signals — a cue over `MAX_CUE_CHARS` (150), or one ending in `?`/`!` (addressed speech). Deliberately NOT a sentence-boundary test: calibrating one against the live store immediately ate `coffee, American vs. Israeli perspectives, quality standards`, and a rule that eats a good cue is worse than one that misses a bad one (the bad one is caught next run; the good one is gone). 27 of 680 flagged, no false positives. Used twice: as **prevention** (`sanitize`, applied at each of the writer's three trigger-write sites) and as a **purge** of records written before it existed (`plan_trigger_purge` → `ReflectionWriter.write_trigger_purge` → `reflection_runner._run_trigger_purge`). Nothing is deleted — a purge re-inserts the record under its own key (`content_key` hashes content only, so it supersedes rather than forks) with the bad cues gone, falling back to embedding on its content when no cue survives. GPU-free self-test: `python -m core.trigger_hygiene`.
- **`server/inference/core/reflection_memory.py`** — `ReflectionMemory`: read-only fold of the append-only `rag_memory.jsonl` op-log (`insert` / `evict` / `surface`) into the current live item set (applies `evict`, dedups by `key`, counts `surface` ops into a per-item `surface_count`). `live_items()` feeds the RAG reflection index (asks embed on their question, facts on their `trigger`, persona on their content — this now includes the weights-bound `[persona]`/`[fact]` mirrored in by the writer, so self-statements are recalled at chat time); `open_questions()` feeds unresolved `[ask]`s back into Sleep (filtered to `kind=="ask"`, so persona/fact recall items are never mis-surfaced as questions); `surfaceable_questions()` selects open `meta`/`user` asks to proactively raise in live chat (meta exempt from the surface-count ceiling, user retires past it); `asks_surfaced_in(session)` joins the `surface` op-log's `surfaced_in` back to the open asks, recovering which questions Ava raised in a given session so the consolidation pass's resolve-and-distill can route their answers. **This closes the reflection→RAG loop:** consolidation conclusions are recalled at chat time, open questions are re-posed each Sleep run so they can eventually be resolved (evicted), and unresolved `meta`/`user` questions are surfaced into new sessions so Ava raises them on her own initiative. `recollection_for(session)` returns a chat's live `[recollection]` so a revisit can supersede it; `embed_text` retrieves a recollection by its `trigger` exactly as it does a fact (that branch keys on `kind in ("fact", "recollection")` — a new kind added without touching it would silently embed on its display text instead).
- **`server/inference/core/reflection_digest.py`** — Persona evidence fold and synthesis. Committed ledger evidence is grouped by the normal map-reduce/LLM clustering path (older MiniLM/exact-key fallbacks remain), weighted by recency, geometric tenure discount (`0.6`), and counter-evidence (`0.5` gain). The raw-evidence fingerprint gates regeneration. Clean-base clustering precedes adapter-authored synthesis; the runner's branch judge uses the existing digest before the new one is synthesized. Polarity screening splits opposing members of merged themes and supplies ledger counter plans; synthesis sees opposition/contested evidence and screens escalating `not:` clauses. The judge can override only with ≥2 themes at `weighted_recurrence >= 1.9`; raw counts are provenance. Live chat reads the active persona's flat `digest.json`, injecting established voice/dispositions/lines and falling back to persona RAG when no portrait is available. General cross-theme opposition detection remains partial. Self-test: `python -m core.reflection_digest`.

### WebSocket protocol

| Client → Server | Purpose |
|---|---|
| `generate` | `{message, user, ...params}` — session-managed; server appends to history, queries RAG, logs. `user` is the speaker's name: injected into the system prompt so Ava knows who is talking, and stored per-exchange (chat log + RAG) so she remembers who told her what. Memory stays shared/global — `user` only labels, never scopes. On the **first turn** of a session the server may also inject a proactive block of open reflection questions (`meta`/`user`) for Ava to raise herself. |
| `generate_ephemeral` | `{conversation, ...params}` — stateless |
| `start_reflection_run` | `{sessions, overrides?, stages?, train_params?, source?, clear_staging?, ...}` — **the live Sleep entry point.** Dispatches the server-side `ReflectionRunner` (consolidation→revision→optional branch) off-thread; the run writes to the staging workspace and streams progress events. `stages` is any of `reflection`/`merge-rag`/`commit-training`/`train`/`persona`; the `train` stage hands LoRA production off to the watchdog's generic job runner (`POST /job/train`) as the run's last act, after finalizing on disk (the watchdog then stops/retrains/relaunches the server). **`persona` is the train-less way to mint the run's Ava version** (Sleep tab's *Train (LoRA)* checkbox unchecked → `[…, "persona"]` instead of `[…, "train"]`): it calls `snapshot_state.produce_persona(run_id, activate=True)` directly with the adapter **unchanged**. It exists because live chat reads her self-portrait through the ACTIVE-PERSONA pointer (`generation._current_chat_portrait` → `persona_paths.active_persona_dir()/digest.json`), NOT from the live `hot/persona/` dir a run writes to — every other product of a run (memory, facts, sidecar targets, anchors, user portraits) is read from its live dir and lands the moment merge-rag/commit-training commits, but the self-portrait is gated on a version being minted, and until 2026-08-12 the only minter was `train_cycle`'s promotion tail. So a train-less run used to refresh her self-portrait into a file nothing read. GPU-free/filesystem-only (safe with the model loaded) and best-effort — a failed snapshot emits `phase_error` and leaves the previous persona active rather than failing a committed reflection. Stripped from a `revisit` alongside `train`, both being version-PRODUCING and a revisit being maintenance on one old chat (it also runs as the head-phase of every normal run). Note the reverse was never true: aborting a training cycle does **not** discard a reflection — the run commits, archives, discards staging and finalizes on disk *before* the watchdog is asked to train, and every build fits a fresh LoRA over the whole frozen-bundle corpus, so an aborted cycle costs GPU time only. `overrides` carries prompts, sampling, etc. (incl. `trigger_purge` — strip pasted-sentence recall cues out of the live trigger-indexed store before the clean-base window, default on, see `core.trigger_hygiene`; `user_notes` — run the per-session "what did I learn about this person?" pass, default on — and `force_user_portrait`, the user-side counterpart of `force_persona_digest`; `self_notes` — run the per-session OUTSIDE-view pass writing `[self_impression]`, default on, see `core.self_portrait` — and `force_self_portrait`, its regenerate override; `chat_facts` — write the per-chat fact-extraction protocol to `<stem>.facts.json`, default on, see `core.chat_facts`; writes a source protocol consumed by the facts-tree build and associative feed; turning it off prevents refreshing that source record); optional `train_params` (`{lora_r?, epochs?, lr?, model_id?, skip_validation?, include_fresh?}`) is mapped to CLI flags by the inference server and forwarded as the job's `args` (`skip_validation` — UI default; currently **moot**: validation is globally disabled via `training/validation_switch.py`, so `train_cycle` force-skips the probe regardless; `include_fresh` — render not-yet-eligible chats as untrained `preview: true` rows for early Training-review repair: on a `train` run it maps to `--include-fresh` (rows appended to the real build's render); on a train-less committed run the service instead writes a GPU-free **preview snapshot** via `training/preview_build.py` (`preview` phase events; outcome `"preview"`, nothing trained) so review can precede the real cycle — see the Sleep tab's *Include fresh chats* checkbox). A normal run also runs a **revisit head-phase** first (unless `revisit_head:false`): it auto-picks one aged chat, re-derives it under the current persona as a silent sub-run, and commits that revisit to live memory so it is RAG context for the main reflection (see the `reflection_service.py` entry). Skipped for a `revisit:true` run and dry runs. **`dry_run: true`** is a write-nothing preview (no staging/memory/ledger/RAG/downstream stages): by default consolidation-only (Sleep tab's *Short Summary*), or with **`dry_full: true`** the full consolidation + revision + branch experiment (Sleep tab's *Dry Sleep*) — the runner streams the summary/IDEAL/branch output and the structured `report` but persists nothing (the clean-base branch judge is skipped, since it is logged-only and would rewrite the sidecar). **`revisit: true`** ("Revisit old chat" button): with `sessions` empty the server picks one random chat ≥ `revisit.min_age_days` (default 7) **and not itself revisited within `revisit.min_revisit_days` (default 7)** (the anti-fixation gate, folded from `data/hot/revisit/revisited.jsonl` so picks rotate; recorded regardless of outcome) and re-reflects it under the *current* persona to re-derive its target — bypassing the reflect-once freeze in place (sidecar rewritten with the re-derived target), suppressing persona formation + prompt modification (no `[persona]`, no digest, no prompt-mutation pass), skipping ingestion, the train hand-off, and **branching** (a revisit trains the freshly re-derived IDEAL directly under current weights rather than replaying the chat's stale tension points; branching stays exclusive to first-time reflection of new chats, so no branch judge runs either). The ack carries `chosen_session`; the picked chat's sidecar is backed up (`.revisit-bak`) and restored if the run doesn't complete. |
| `reflection_run_status` / `reflection_run_events` / `stop_reflection_run` / `list_reflection_runs` / `get_reflection_run` | Monitor / control a reflection run: poll status, fetch buffered progress events (`after_seq`), request a stop, list runs, fetch one run's full record. |
| `activity_events` | `{after_seq}` — fetch unified **activity-journal** events with `seq > after_seq` (0 = the whole ring). The single, always-on stream every autonomous/GPU subsystem writes to (wander/outreach/synthesis/checkin/background_reflection lifecycles + a reflection mirror — phase grain for operator runs, full Sleep-tab detail with chat name + pass output for background per-chat runs); the Activity tab polls it with one cursor, independent of any run. See `core.activity_log`. | Optional `levels` narrows to the record levels the client renders (`event`/`body`/`stream`/`raw`; absent ⇒ all). The batch is byte-capped newest-first, since a `body` record carries a whole generation.
| `get_worklog` | `{after_seq?}` — fetch **first-person episodic worklog** entries with `id > after_seq` (0 = all). Ava's durable, semantic record of what she did (one entry per episode, in her own voice), distinct from the activity journal. Replies `worklog_batch`. The Worklog tab polls it with one id cursor; nothing acts on the worklog yet (preview only). See `core.worklog`. |
| `prompt_rewrite_status` | `{attempts?}` — **Prompt tab, the autonomous rewrite's window**: the currency gate's verdict chain (`core.prompt_rewrite.offer` walked past the model check, with the first failing reason, the hours left on the gap, the deltas logged since the last attempt, the mature patterns and the exact line the executive would be shown), the settings in force, the pattern budget as `patterns.json` left it, and the newest `attempts` (default 5) records from `rewrite_log.jsonl` in full — timestamp, outcome, chosen candidate, `WHY`, patterns weighed, consumed keys, every candidate's text. Read-only, GPU-free. Replies `prompt_rewrite_status`. |
| `get_reflection_prompts` | `{}` — fetch the server's current sleep/revision/branch prompt text (for the UI's editable overrides). |
| `branch_exchange` | `{filename, exchange_index, temperature, top_p}` — replay a logged exchange's contested answer tokens (needs the tension extension's `token_ids` + `alt_token_id`), force each road-not-taken, continue under current weights, embedder-filter degenerate branches. Read-only; returns candidates. The runner calls this internally when branching is enabled. |
| `match_anchors` | `{text, limit?}` — **diagnostic**: which stored per-exchange anchors would *text* match? Lexical tag overlap only (no model, no embedder, no GPU), inflection-tolerant. Replies `anchor_matches {text, matches, corpus}` where each match carries `session`/`exchange_index`/`about`/`tags`/`matched`/`score`. Retrieves and injects nothing; backs the Chat tab's **Match preview** strip so the query side of anchor retrieval can be judged against real text before any of it is wired into chat. |
| `get_open_questions` | `{}` — request reflection's still-open `[ask]` items |
| `update_persona` / `update_facts` | `{baseline_keys, retained_keys}` — **Persona / Facts tab cleanup**: evict the live `[persona]` / `[fact]` items the operator removed from the fetched set. Two kinds of one store, so one server implementation (`server._apply_memory_eviction`, parameterized by kind). `baseline_keys` is an optimistic-concurrency fence against the live set *of that kind* — a reflection that landed since the fetch is a rejected `conflict`, not a silently stale edit. Appends `op:"evict"` records (`reason` `persona_editor`/`facts_editor`) to `rag_memory.jsonl` + `evict` tombstones to the consolidation ledger, then refreshes RAG in place; runnable snapshots, reflection archives, adapter weights and the current digest are never touched. Refused while a reflection/generation job owns the box. Replies `persona_updated` / `facts_updated`. |
| `outreach_now` | `{}` — **debug (Sleep tab "Reach Out")**: manually run one Ava-initiated outreach decision (the same pass the idle heartbeat runs) and stream her reasoning back. Streams `outreach_question` then `outreach_chunk`, terminating with `outreach_done`. A yes writes the reversed session into the chats list; a resolved (she already learned the answer) formally evicts the open ask instead. |
| `list_modules` | `{}` — the **module registry** (Modules tab): one entry per pass with its `name`/`label`, the `prompt` text as it stands on disk, its `injectables` catalogue (empty so far), what it produces, and its **`source`** — the input KIND it reads, which the client turns into a `list_module_inputs` call rather than knowing per module. Six modules: `chat_facts`, `chat_summary`, `til_facts`, `fact_fetch`, `assoc_fetch` (the associative library's stage 1) and `assoc_pivot` (its word-anchor context switch, §6) — see `core/assoc_bridge.py`. Replies `modules_list {modules}`. See `core.modules`. |
| `list_module_inputs` | `{source}` — **what a module of this input kind can be run against** (Modules tab). Keyed on the SOURCE rather than the module, so the two chat-reading modules share one listing and one fetch: `chat` returns transcripts (path-guarded to the chats dir), `til` the fetched articles + news digests under `data/til/snippets/<kind>/`, each as `{id, label, sub, …}`. A separate RPC from `list_modules` because the registry is three small entries while the input list grows with the corpus. Replies `module_inputs {source, inputs}`. |
| `run_module` | `{module, filename, prompt?, inject?, temperature?, top_p?}` — **run ONE module against ONE chat** (Modules tab "Simulate"). **WRITES NOTHING**: the produced value is returned, not committed — a `chat_facts` simulation never touches `<stem>.facts.json`. `prompt` overrides the module's on-disk prompt **for this run only** (the file is never written). v0 injects nothing (`disable_rag=True`, no persona, no clock) and a non-empty `inject` is **refused** (`unsupported_injectable`) rather than ignored. Refused while another GPU job holds the box, and with no model loaded. Streams `module_stage` / `module_chunk`, terminating with `module_done`. A chunked module emits one `module_stage {stage:"generating", part, parts}` per block. |
| `deliberate_now` | `{n?, execute?}` — **manual deliberation (Worklog tab "Deliberate")**: Ava reads her recent episodic worklog (`n` entries, default 20) + open threads and decides what to do next (wander / synthesize / reach_out / check_in / revisit / aha / pivot / nothing — plus `rewrite_prompt` when `core.prompt_rewrite.offer()` has put it on her menu; chosen unoffered it is refused as `dispatch_skipped: not_offered`, and an offered-but-not-chosen one is a *declined* attempt). Streams `deliberation_stage` then `deliberation_chunk` (reasoning, `<think>` included), terminating with `deliberation_done`. Dry run by default — the terminal message reports the action she WOULD take; `execute: true` dispatches it exactly as the autonomous `deliberation` idle job does (the Worklog tab's button never sets it). See `core.deliberation`. |
| `checkin_now` | `{sim_hours?, user?}` — **debug/simulate (Sleep tab "Check In")**: manually run one check-in decision for ONE person (the same per-person pass the idle sweep runs after `checkin.silence_threshold_hours` of *their* silence) with the silence period **forged** so it fires regardless of real elapsed time (`sim_hours` overrides the forged hours; the server defaults it to the threshold, clamped up to the real silence). `user` names whose check-in it is — their silence clock, their recent conversations, their opener — defaulting to the active session's speaker (the client sends the Chat tab's user box). One decision, not the whole sweep: several people's deliberations streaming into one log would be unreadable, and watching one is the point of the button. She reviews her recent conversations with that person and decides whether to reach out to them. Streams `checkin_stage` then `checkin_chunk`, terminating with `checkin_done`. A yes writes the reversed session into the chats list. |
| `dedup_facts` | `{dry_run?}` — **semantic `[fact]` merge (Sleep/Debug tab "Dedup facts")**: the manual, live-writing sibling of the pass a reflection run performs at the end of its clean-base window. Folds the live facts, blocks them by SUBJECT (recall-cue embedding on the CPU — the load-bearing step, since a grouping call sees one block and so blocking order decides what may merge at all; see `core/fact_dedup.py`), groups paraphrases by meaning on the **CLEAN base** (adapter off — an evaluation, not Ava's expression), then keeps one survivor per group carrying the UNION of the group's recall cues and evicts the rest. `dry_run` (default true) previews without writing. Streams `dedup_stage` per block, terminating with `facts_deduped`. Facts only; persona/asks untouched. |
| `digest_dryrun` | `{block_size?, temperature?}` — **DRY RUN (Sleep tab "Persona digest (dry)")**: re-derive the persona digest end to end and return it, **writing nothing** — no digest snapshot, no `current` pointer move, no RAG refresh, no ledger write. Clusters live `[persona]` evidence into themes on the **CLEAN base** (adapter off — grouping paraphrases is an evaluation, mirroring `fact_dedup`/`self_reconcile`; the live pass instead clusters on the adapter) via `core.persona_cluster.run_map_reduce`, then synthesizes the self-portrait on the **ADAPTER** (authorship stays with Ava). The regenerate fingerprint gate is bypassed — an operator asking for a preview means it. Streams `digest_dryrun_stage` (gathered / clean_base_enter / map / reduce_started / reduce / clustered / synthesizing) then `digest_dryrun_chunk`, terminating with `digest_dryrun_done`. |
| `regen_persona` | `{block_size?, temperature?}` — **Persona tab "Regen persona"**: the WRITE counterpart of `digest_dryrun`. Same pass on the same models (plan forced — the operator asked; cluster on the **CLEAN base**; synthesize on the **ADAPTER**), but the digest is **written** to the live persona store through `reflection_digest.synthesize_digest` (the reflection run's own write seam, self-portrait included) and the run's Ava version is then minted + **activated** via `snapshot_state.produce_persona(run_id="regen_<ts>", activate=True)` — the Sleep `persona` stage's call, so the refreshed self-portrait reaches the next live turn. Adapter unchanged; previous persona snapshots stay as rollback; the snapshot step is best-effort (a failure leaves the digest written and the PREVIOUS persona active, reported via `activated: false` + `persona_error`). Streams `regen_persona_stage` / `regen_persona_chunk`, terminating with `regen_persona_done`. |
| `clear_context` | `{user}` — reset server-side conversation and start a new log session tagged with the speaker. Counts as activity on the idle clock (2026-09-03), so the chat it releases is not reflected in the background the moment the operator is back. A genuine socket disconnect releases the active chat too (`session_ops.release_active_session`, no new session started) so it is reflectable while the user is away |
| `retry_last_exchange` | `{}` — **Chat "Retry"** (completed-turn sibling of `cancel(discard=True)`): roll the latest completed exchange off the active transcript + in-memory conversation so a collapsed/degenerate reply never reaches reflection/training, and return its user prompt to resend under adjusted sampling. Refused on a live reflection run, an already-reflected session, an empty session, or a synthetic Ava opener (no real user turn). The active session is RAG-fenced, so the reply was never indexed. Replies `retry_ready {user_prompt, speaker, exchange_index}`. |
| `set_session_notes` | `{notes}` — attach operator notes to the active session |
| `list_sessions` / `get_session` / `load_session` | `{filename, in_place?}` — list completed sessions, fetch one's JSON, or adopt one as the active conversation. `load_session` normally *forks* (starts a fresh `continued_from` file, keeping the original immutable); with `in_place: true` it **resumes the same file** (`ChatLogger.resume_session`) so replies append to that transcript — used to answer an Ava-initiated outreach in its own file, keeping the reversed conversation one reflectable unit. **Un-freeze on continue:** an in-place resume of an already-reflected chat clears its `reflected_at`/`chat_reflected` freeze (`ChatSidecar.clear_reflection_freeze`) and purges its background per-chat staging (`reflection_staging.purge_background_artifacts`), so turns appended after the freeze re-enter the reflection backlog instead of being lost — the fork-on-continue that protects normal chats doesn't apply to in-place-resumed ones. |
| `reset_session_reflection` | `{filename}` — **Chat "Re-reflect chat"**: delete a past chat's `.state.json` sidecar so the chat re-reflects from scratch. The sidecar IS the reflection of a chat, so this one act drops the reflect-once freeze (`reflected_at`/`chat_reflected`), the per-exchange verdicts + trainable targets, the consolidation summary/gist and the retrieval anchors; the transcript is untouched and the next run (foreground or the background per-chat pass) re-derives all of it under the *current* persona. The whole-chat deliberate sibling of the automatic single-exchange invalidation `mark_corrupt` performs and of the un-freeze `load_session in_place` performs. Purges the chat's background per-chat staging in the same act (`reflection_staging.purge_background_artifacts`) — otherwise the next normal run copies the checkpoint's mirror of the frozen sidecar back over live and re-freezes it. Path-guarded to hot/chats; refused during a reflection run; the ACTIVE conversation is allowed (the live logger writes the transcript, never the sidecar). **Destructive in one named way:** human-`locked` Training-review repairs live in that file and go with it. Replies `session_reflection_reset {filename, removed, had_sidecar}`. |
| `mark_corrupt` | `{filename, exchange_index, corrupt_cot?, corrupt_response?}` — flag a past exchange's stored CoT and/or reply as corrupt (automatic-repair path; the Training review tab now uses `regenerate_exchange` instead). Writes the optional per-exchange `corrupt_cot`/`corrupt_response` booleans onto the source chat JSON (schema v4); each flag is applied only when present, so the two toggles are independent. Path-guarded to hot/chats; refuses the active session + a live reflection run, allows a frozen one. Setting a flag **deletes the chat's poisoned sidecar** (un-freezes it) so it re-reflects clean; the un-frozen chat stops training immediately. Reflection then treats a corrupt CoT as missing (and, when the reply is kept, regenerates its `<think>` via a similarity-gated re-answer grafted onto the trusted reply — approach #3) / insists on a re-derived IDEAL for a corrupt reply, and the build drops the corruption. Replies `exchange_corrupt_marked` (with `sidecar_invalidated`). |
| `regenerate_exchange` | `{filename, exchange_index, temperature, system_suffix?, cot_only?, adapter?}` — **Training review "Regenerate"**: re-answer one logged exchange with the **currently loaded adapter** — or, with `adapter` (a bare dir name from the review payload's `adapters` lineage, resolved + path-guarded under the server's own `models/` by `generation._resolve_regen_adapter`), with an **alternate adapter swapped in — STICKY** (`agentic.swap_model`: one-way release → load, ONE reload, and it then stays loaded, so a repair session's further regenerations under the same choice swap nothing — the box deliberately keeps running that adapter afterwards, chat and idle jobs included, honestly reported by `status`; a Chat-tab load or restart undoes it, the config's `adapter_id` untouched. A failed load restores the previous model — the shared failure discipline. `regenerate_status {text}` milestones narrate a reload when one happens, and `generation._regen_swap_active` fences the swap window — chat/ephemeral refuse, gossip/API `is_busy` — since the runtime transiently holds no model during the release→load) (`generation.handle_regenerate_exchange` → executor thread, via the reflection IDEAL seam `build_ideal_messages` + `_make_sync_reflect_generate`). Rebuilds the exact pre-answer conversation and generates a fresh `<think>…</think>` reply. **The thought opener is always prefilled** (`force_think` → `fam.think_prefill` + `min_think_tokens`, mirroring the live-chat `_run_generation` path) so the re-answer can't come back CoT-less — the IDEAL seam rebuilds a CoT-stripped prior conversation, which otherwise drives the sampled `<think>`-opener probability to zero and lets the model answer with no thought block. `cot_only` (the Corrupt-CoT box checked WITHOUT Corrupt-reply, so Apply grafts only the new `<think>` onto the kept reply) halts generation the moment the reasoning channel closes (`fam.close_markers`) instead of spending the token budget on a reply nothing reads. Optional `system_suffix` is an operator-supplied delivery constraint from the tab's **Suffix** box, appended to the system prompt for **this generation only** (not the trainable prefix persisted on the anchor — so the row trains clean while the re-answer follows the steer, e.g. away from a reflexive trailing question). Read-only preview — writes nothing; refuses while a reflection run / encounter owns the GPU. **Streams and is stoppable:** the re-answer is pushed out as `regenerate_chunk {cot, reply}` deltas (server-side classification via `_CotStreamSplitter`, so the family's raw markers never reach the UI) and terminated by `exchange_regenerated {new_cot, new_reply, original_cot, original_reply, cancelled}` — the authoritative pair, from the cleaned text. A `cancel` sent mid-generation halts it within a step and returns the partial with `cancelled: true` (the handler drains `msg_queue` like the chat path, putting every non-`cancel` message back); a partial cut mid-thought is classified from the splitter's state, since no close marker reached the raw text. |
| `apply_regenerated_exchange` | `{filename, exchange_index, corrupt_cot, corrupt_response, new_cot, new_reply, original_reply}` — persist a reviewed regeneration **straight to the chat sidecar** (`session_ops.handle_apply_regenerated_exchange`). Assembles a faithful target — `corrupt_response` ⇒ `<think>{new_cot}</think>{new_reply}` (a regenerated reply carries its own new CoT); `corrupt_cot` only ⇒ `<think>{new_cot}</think>{kept_reply}` where `kept_reply` is the client-sent `original_reply` (the answer the tab is DISPLAYING = the current trained target's reply, which differs from the raw transcript `assistant_response` for an empty-CoT/revised-target row) — the new CoT is grafted onto the reply the operator sees, and the freshly regenerated reply is discarded; falls back to `assistant_response` if `original_reply` is absent — writes it via `ChatSidecar.write_verdict` as `target_source:"revised"` / `target_kind:"manual_regen"` / `target_generation:"chat_manual_regen_v1"` / `locked:true`, **locks the exchange** (per-exchange human-validation — re-reflection AND revisit skip it, so the reviewed target survives; the rest of the chat still reflects normally), and clears any lingering corrupt flags on the exchange. Path-guarded to hot/chats; refuses the active session + a live reflection run. Replies `exchange_regenerated_applied {target_kind, applied_cot, reply_source, new_cot, new_reply}`. |
| `set_training_ban` | `{target, exchange_index, banned}` — **Training review "Ban from training"**: bar one row from every future build, or lift the ban. *target* is the row's `source_session` — a chat transcript filename (with its `exchange_index`), or `wander:<ts>` for a wander capture (index `null`). The flag lands on the exchange's sidecar record / the wander corpus record; `training.dialogue_source.build_dialogue_anchor` and `core.wander_sft.load_pending` refuse a banned row, so it leaves the corpus on the next build (a banned wander also leaves the chat-RAG wander channel). The source itself survives — reversible — until `rewrite_history` deletes it. Refuses on a live reflection run and on the ACTIVE chat (its logger's next save would drop the flag). Replies `training_ban_set {target, exchange_index, banned, kind}`. |
| `rewrite_history` | `{}` — **Training review "Rewrite history"**: the finalize counterpart to Apply. For **every** human-reviewed (`locked`) exchange in hot/chats (`session_ops.handle_rewrite_history`), bake the reviewed sidecar target back into the *transcript* — rewrite `assistant_cot`/`assistant_response` (schema v5, via `chat_logger.rewrite_exchange_history`), preserving the prior content under an append-only per-exchange `rewrite_history` record and **deleting** the now-stale `tension` block (its per-token logits/`token_ids` were measured on the original generation, so branch replay would replay stale ids), and clear corrupt flags — then **unfreeze** the exchange (`ChatSidecar.set_exchange_locked(..., False)`, the one intentional lock-clearing path). Training output is unchanged (`dialogue_source` reads the target off the untouched sidecar); what's fixed is the transcript-derived data — RAG recall (chat index re-embedded after), snapshots, and every later exchange's context.
