Loaded by `deliberation._load_prompt`; overridable on disk. |
| `server/inference/prompts/checkin_prompt.txt` | Check-in decision prompt — after `{hours}` of user silence, review the `{recent}` recaps of recent conversations and decide whether to reach out to `{user}` on her own accord (`DECISION: yes/no` + `OPENER`). Loaded by `checkin._load_prompt`; overridable on disk. |
| `server/inference/prompts/checkin_summary_prompt.txt` | Check-in per-chat recap prompt — compresses ONE recent conversation (`{when}`/`{user}`/`{transcript}`) into a 2-3 sentence recap (`RECAP:`), so five chats that don't fit one window are each represented before the decision pass. Loaded by `checkin._load_summary_prompt`; overridable on disk. |
| `server/inference/prompts/revisit_prompt.txt` | Revision-pass prompt for the **"Revisit old chat"** pass — same VERDICT/WHY/LANG_DRIFT/IDEAL contract as `revision_prompt.txt` but framed as remembering a past exchange under the evolved persona and **without a PERSONA field** (persona formation is suppressed). Loaded by `reflection_service`/`reflection_run.py` into `overrides.revision_prompt` when `revisit` is set. |
| `server/inference/prompts/branch_prompt.txt` | Branch-select prompt — blind choice among counterfactual replies (loaded by `reflection_prompts.py`; overridable per run) |
| `server/inference/prompts/persona_digest_prompt.txt` | Persona-digest synthesis prompt — directs Ava to fold her persona evidence into the four-facet self-portrait (loaded by `reflection_digest.load_digest_prompt`) |
| `server/inference/prompts/persona_cluster_prompt.txt` | Persona-clustering prompt — the LLM grouping pass that merges paraphrases/cross-lingual restatements of one disposition into a theme (`GROUP: <numbers>` output, loaded by `reflection_digest.load_cluster_prompt`; MiniLM/exact-key fallback when unavailable) |
| `server/inference/prompts/branch_judge_prompt.txt` | Digest-aware branch-judge prompt (phase-two judge channel, weighted-maturity-gated override) — same blind option set as `branch_prompt`, but the criterion is "which reply is who I'm becoming?" with a `{persona}` slot filled by `reflection_digest.render_digest_for_judge` (loaded by `load_branch_judge_prompt`) |
| `server/inference/prompts/fact_placement_prompt.txt` | Fact-placement judge prompt (phase-two clean-base pass) — a `{fact}` slot + letter-labelled candidate exchanges; the judge picks the ONE whose reasoning rests on the fact, or "None". Loaded by `reflection_runner._load_fact_placement_prompt` |
| `server/inference/data/hot/reflection_staging/` | Staged Sleep workspace: chats / memory / consolidation / archive deltas written by a reflection run before `merge-rag`/`commit-training`/`apply` promote them to live. A `STAGED_BY.json` pointer records the run id(s) that produced the workspace (appended under continue-staging), so a standalone stage command attributes itself back to the right run(s) in the reflection log. |
| `server/inference/data/hot/reflection_checkpoint/` | Durable per-chat completion checkpoint (outside `reflection_staging/` so `clear_staging` never wipes it): a foreground run mirrors each completed chat's frozen sidecar + cumulative deltas here for crash recovery; the **background per-chat pass** appends each completed chat's `chat_reflected` sidecar + one-chat deltas here (accumulating across wakes). The next normal run folds it into live at start and deletes it. |
| `server/inference/data/hot/reflection_pending_clean_base/` | `<stem>.json` per background-reflected chat: the deferred clean-base **job payloads** (`judge_jobs`/`fact_candidates`) a later normal run loads (consume-once) to finish a `chat_reflected` chat. A **sibling** of the checkpoint so the fold's `discard_checkpoint` rmtree can't delete jobs the main pass hasn't consumed. |
| `server/inference/data/hot/background_staging/` | The background per-chat pass's throwaway staging workspace — one chat at a time, reset each iteration and discarded after commit. Isolated from `reflection_staging/` so it can never collide with a foreground continue-staging / clear-staging run. |
| `server/inference/data/hot/reflection_runs/` | **Reflection log**, per run id: `<run_id>.meta.json` (mutable run state + progress), `<run_id>.events.jsonl` (progress events), `<run_id>.summary.json` (final counters), and `<run_id>.report.json` (detailed run statistics). These files are copied into the review archive; no replay provenance or stage journal is stored. |
| `server/inference/data/hot/activity/` | **Unified activity journal** — `activity.jsonl`, the single append-only box-wide log (one global monotonic restart-durable `seq`) every autonomous/GPU subsystem writes to via `core/activity_log.py`. Ring-bounded in memory + size-rotated on disk; served to the Activity tab via `activity_events`. | Rotated into numbered segments (`activity.<seq>.jsonl`) rather than truncated, so history survives; carries the `body`/`stream`/`raw` levels as well as lifecycle events, and is written by the offline train cycle too. **Excluded from runnable snapshots** — it records what the source box DID, not what Ava knows.
| `server/inference/data/hot/reachout/` | **Reach-out tombstones** — `expired.jsonl`, one append-only line (`stem`, `user`, `sent_at`, `opener`, `expired_at`) per unanswered opener the stale-reach-out sweep deleted from the corpus (`core/chat_worklog.delete_stale_reachouts`). Not a memory Ava reads: it exists so the three guards that count messages she sent and was ignored on — the reach-out backoff (`reachout_gate.unanswered_streak`), check-in's standing-opener list, and outreach's dangling-opener guard — survive the transcript's deletion, which would otherwise weaken each of them precisely as the silence lengthened. Wiped **with the chats** (`state_wipe.wipe_chats_and_archive`), not with regenerable state: while the corpus lives, these are part of what it records. |
| `server/inference/data/hot/prompt/` | Standing-prompt state: `experiment.json` (the live experimental prompt, restart-surviving, see `core/prompt_experiment.py`), `experiment_log.jsonl`, `prompt_deltas.jsonl` (the prompt-mutation pass's logged proposals — each with `locator`/`tension_rank`/`tension_key` since 2026-09-18) `tension_baseline_cache.json` (derived: per-transcript CoT-tension stats keyed on mtime+size, so `core/tension_baseline.py` re-parses only changed transcripts; disposable), `patterns.json` (derived: the deltas folded into patterns with their tension-weighted recurrence and maturity, rebuilt by every normal reflection run's clean-base batch — `core/prompt_patterns.py`; what the deliberation executive reads) `rewrite_log.jsonl` (the append-only attempt record — `outcome` changed/stayed/declined, `chosen`, `why`, `patterns_weighed`, `consumed_keys`, the candidate texts, `worklog_id`; what the gate and the pattern fold read) and `rewrite/<event_id>/` (one dir per event: `sample_<k>.txt` + `.raw.txt`, `consensus.txt`, `final.txt`, `choice.raw.txt`, and `event.json` while in flight — the resume marker). |
| `server/inference/data/hot/worklog/` | **First-person episodic worklog** — `worklog.jsonl`, the durable append-only record of what Ava did, one entry per meaningful episode in her own voice (durable id, kind, first-person `summary`, `refs` into source traces, optional `opens`/`closes` loop fold). Written by `core/worklog.py` at each episode-close; kept forever (not a ring); served to the Worklog preview tab via `get_worklog`. |
| `server/reflections/<run_id>/` | **Reflection review archive**: run logs, committed artifact deltas, persona digest, and an adapter copy when training ran. It has no manifest or replay index. |
| `server/server.log` | Inference server stdout/stderr (plus watchdog-written training lifecycle lines, since the inference process is down during training) |
| `server/train.log` | Offline `train_cycle` subprocess stdout/stderr (full, verbose) |
| `server/train_progress.jsonl` | Structured per-stage train-cycle progress (truncated per cycle), written by `training/train_progress.py`, served via the watchdog's `GET /job/progress` for the Sleep tab's live training view |
| `server/watchdog.log` | Watchdog process stdout/stderr |
| `~/.avachat/error.log` | Uncaught exception log |
